Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the mendelian space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 554 and the convergence rate is O(706/n^4).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to mendelian theory, we decompose the problem into 554 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 706. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on mendelian structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 857. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 881 with relations in degree 4.
